	focus = {
		id = PRF_final_struggle
		icon = GFX_goal_india_princely_federation
		cost = 7.00
		x = 17
		y = 18

		available = {
			date > 1937.1.1
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus PRF_final_struggle"
			add_war_support = 0.20
		}
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_WAR_SUPPORT }
	}

	focus = {
		id = PRF_fortify_north_border
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_india_fortify_against_raj
		cost = 15.00
		prerequisite = {
			focus = PRF_final_struggle
		}
		prerequisite = {
			focus = PRF_army_reform
		}
		relative_position_id = PRF_final_struggle
		x = -7
		y = 1

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = DEH }
			NOT = { has_war_with = BHC }
			owns_state = 429 #Bombay
			owns_state = 893 #Nagpur
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}

		bypass = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { owns_state = 429 } #Bombay
				NOT = { owns_state = 893 } #Nagpur
				owns_state = 428 #Gujarat
				owns_state = 437 #Indore
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
